## VTSE-004 — VTSE-004 — P1 — Multi-item refund reverses excessive commission and has two reversal paths

- Original report: [docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md:100](../../docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md#L100); type: forensic finding / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: a full or partial payment refund where the payment covers multiple booking items.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: two items with different totals, partial and full refunds, retry of the event, and assertions that each reversal is capped and aggregate debits equal the refunded allocation exactly once.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: **Trigger:** a full or partial payment refund where the payment covers multiple booking items. **Evidence:** [ProcessRefundAction.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Application/Actions/ProcessRefundAction.php#L93) directly reverses the first commission using the complete refund amount, then emits `RefundCompleted`; [ReverseCommissionOnRefundCompletedListener.php](../../app/Modules/Settlement/Application/Listeners/ReverseCommissionOnRefundCompletedListener.php#L80) applies the same full amount to every commission; [ReverseCommissionAction.php](../../app/Modules/Settlement/Application/Actions/ReverseCommissionAction.php#L39) divides the full refund by each item's gross and does not cap reversal to the remaining vendor/platform amounts. **Impact:** vendor balances and commission receivables can be over-reversed. The first commission can also have its state advanced twice even where the ledger idempotency key suppresses a duplicate ledger group.
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: vendor; candidate modules: Booking, Payments, Settlement. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.
- Required fixture data: disposable records matching the reported trigger above, including its lifecycle, age, ownership, localization, and monetary values; fields omitted by the report remain unresolved. Do not substitute empty fixtures for populated workflow records.
- Related scenario IDs: SC-Settlement-3f9c8b9342, SC-Settlement-9b39fde807, SC-Settlement-1a9fd621e4, SC-Settlement-3b8af179fa, SC-Settlement-93e2829b41, SC-Payments-e7d1c7c10f, SC-Payments-ecf87b2d24, SC-Payments-3ea5c1580e, SC-Payments-f587c6fa5f, SC-Payments-ff1b150250. Matching source references are candidate traceability only.
- Current persistence: UNKNOWN — not executed in this pilot; historical remediation is not current proof.
- Related later report references: None found.

## VTSE-005 — VTSE-005 — P1 — Completed refund can call the gateway again

- Original report: [docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md:110](../../docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md#L110); type: forensic finding / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: an API refund completes successfully, then a `REFUND` webhook or queue retry processes the same refund.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: replay a completed refund and assert one gateway call, one ledger group, and a permanently completed state.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: **Trigger:** an API refund completes successfully, then a `REFUND` webhook or queue retry processes the same refund. **Evidence:** [ProcessRefundAction.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Application/Actions/ProcessRefundAction.php#L41) locks the refund but has no completed-state guard before marking it processing and calling the gateway; [ProcessPaymobWebhookAction.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Application/Actions/ProcessPaymobWebhookAction.php#L78) can enter that path. **Impact:** duplicate gateway refund attempt or a valid completed refund being overwritten as failed.
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: system/gateway; candidate modules: Payments, Settlement. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.
- Required fixture data: disposable records matching the reported trigger above, including its lifecycle, age, ownership, localization, and monetary values; fields omitted by the report remain unresolved. Do not substitute empty fixtures for populated workflow records.

## VTSE-007 — VTSE-007 — P1 — Captured webhook amount is not reconciled with the payment

- Original report: [docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md:130](../../docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md#L130); type: forensic finding / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Paymob sends a successful capture event with an amount or currency different from the local payment.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: mismatched amount/currency must fail closed, preserve the payment state, and create no capture ledger.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: **Trigger:** Paymob sends a successful capture event with an amount or currency different from the local payment. **Evidence:** [ProcessPaymobWebhookAction.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Application/Actions/ProcessPaymobWebhookAction.php#L102) invokes capture without validating the parsed captured amount; [CapturePaymentAction.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Application/Actions/CapturePaymentAction.php#L46) posts the full local payment amount. **Impact:** the system can mark and ledger more money than the gateway actually captured.
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: system/gateway; candidate modules: Payments, Settlement. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.

## VTSE-009 — VTSE-009 — P1 — Two settlement paths can pay one withdrawal twice

- Original report: [docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md:150](../../docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md#L150); type: forensic finding / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: the weekly settlement batch and the admin mark-paid flow read the same approved withdrawal before either updates it.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: interleave both paths for one withdrawal and assert a single transition, ledger group, proof policy, and payout reference.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: **Trigger:** the weekly settlement batch and the admin mark-paid flow read the same approved withdrawal before either updates it. **Evidence:** [SettlementRunAction.php](../../app/Modules/Settlement/Application/Actions/SettlementRunAction.php#L29) reads approved withdrawals without a row lock and uses `wd_settle_batch:{id}`; [MarkWithdrawalPaidAction.php](../../app/Modules/Settlement/Application/Actions/MarkWithdrawalPaidAction.php#L42) checks state before its transaction and uses `wd_settle:{id}` near line 100. **Impact:** two valid ledger groups can represent the same payout. The batch path also marks paid without the bank proof, transfer reference, admin identity, and audit evidence required by the manual path.
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: admin; candidate modules: Settlement, Identity. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.
- Required fixture data: disposable records matching the reported trigger above, including its lifecycle, age, ownership, localization, and monetary values; fields omitted by the report remain unresolved. Do not substitute empty fixtures for populated workflow records.
- Related scenario IDs: SC-Settlement-89f80f65fb, SC-Settlement-573325a1d6, SC-Settlement-8fdcef03ff, SC-Settlement-d7d67d29af, SC-Settlement-22924614e5, SC-Settlement-394cd88b83. Matching source references are candidate traceability only.
- Current persistence: UNKNOWN — not executed in this pilot; historical remediation is not current proof.
- Related later report references: None found.

## VTSE-013 — VTSE-013 — P1 — Modification preview computes the wrong quantity delta

- Original report: [docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md:190](../../docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md#L190); type: forensic finding / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: item quantity changes, with or without a unit-price change.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: old `1 × 100`, new `3 × 200`, expected delta `500`; include quantity-only and price-only cases.

## VTSE-019 — VTSE-019 — P1 — Item completion does not complete the parent booking

## VTSE-020 — VTSE-020 — P2 — HTTP idempotency key is not scoped to actor and operation

- Original report: [docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md:260](../../docs/qa/vendor-to-settlement-e2e-audit-2026-09-12.md#L260); type: forensic finding / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: **Evidence:** [IdempotencyKeyMiddleware.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Http/Middleware/IdempotencyKeyMiddleware.php#L25) looks up the supplied key without a complete actor/route scope and does not atomically reserve it before downstream execution. **Impact:** a key reused by another actor/route can return an unrelated response; concurrent first requests can both reach the gateway.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: same key across users/routes and two concurrent requests; only the matching actor/operation may replay.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: **Evidence:** [IdempotencyKeyMiddleware.php](../../app/Modules/Payments/Http/Middleware/IdempotencyKeyMiddleware.php#L25) looks up the supplied key without a complete actor/route scope and does not atomically reserve it before downstream execution. **Impact:** a key reused by another actor/route can return an unrelated response; concurrent first requests can both reach the gateway.

- Required fixture data: disposable records matching the reported trigger above, including its lifecycle, age, ownership, localization, and monetary values; fields omitted by the report remain unresolved. Do not substitute empty fixtures for populated workflow records.
- Related scenario IDs: SC-Payments-2149481247, SC-Payments-001fd64644, SC-Payments-6a1b5c748f, SC-Payments-7362353d44, SC-Payments-972cef51bf, SC-Payments-3dbf716621. Matching source references are candidate traceability only.

## SRCH-01 — P1 — Add Governorate then City or Area dependency to search.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:56](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L56); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The search sidebar previously exposed one unscoped City field, accepted city without governorate outside planner mode, and never passed governorate into the search contract. Added separate Governorate then City controls backed by the existing Geography API: City is disabled until a governorate is selected, receives only that governorate's active cities, clears stale values on parent change, and preserves both values on query-string reload. Web validation now requires the parent for a city and rejects mismatched pairs. `SearchServicesDTO` and the existing database fallback now apply governorate coverage (primary location or covered city) together with the city filter; governorate searches intentionally use the established SQL path instead of expanding the Meilisearch index in this ticket. No migration or stored-data rewrite was required. Evidence: focused Pest 4 tests / 28 assertions, successful Vite production build, and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus English desktop and Arabic mobile dependency/filter flow).
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P1 — Add Governorate then City or Area dependency to search. (reported change target; not independent business approval)

Web validation now requires the parent for a city and rejects mismatched pairs. `SearchServicesDTO` and the existing database fallback now apply governorate coverage (primary location or covered city) together with the city filter; governorate searches intentionally use the established SQL path instead of expanding the Meilisearch index in this ticket. No migration or stored-data rewrite was required. Evidence: focused Pest 4 tests / 28 assertions, successful Vite production build, and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus English desktop and Arabic mobile dependency/filter flow).
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: Not enumerated by source; candidate modules: Catalog, Discovery. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.

## SRCH-02 — P1 — Filter vendor options by selected Occasion and Category.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:58](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L58); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The vendor select previously listed every approved vendor with any published service, regardless of the selected occasion/category. `VendorBrowsingRepository::approvedForFilter()` now requires one published service whose category (including parent-category selection) and linked occasion satisfy both active filters. The search controller removes an invalid submitted vendor instead of applying a stale choice, and the existing sidebar JavaScript refreshes options immediately from server-derived vendor/service combinations whenever either parent changes. No route, taxonomy, migration, or stored-data rewrite was added. Evidence: focused Pest 3 tests / 24 assertions for the vendor and geography filters, successful Vite production build, and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus English desktop and Arabic mobile dynamic vendor options, including stale selection clearing).

No route, taxonomy, migration, or stored-data rewrite was added. Evidence: focused Pest 3 tests / 24 assertions for the vendor and geography filters, successful Vite production build, and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus English desktop and Arabic mobile dynamic vendor options, including stale selection clearing).

## VEND-01 — P2 — Add a stable vendor slug URL.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:62](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L62); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The profile already had a unique, protected slug and ULID identity, but the public controller accepted only ULIDs and generated links ignored the slug. Public Blade vendor links now prefer the existing slug, approved profiles resolve by slug or ULID, and legacy ULID paths return a 301 redirect retaining query parameters. API/model route identity remains ULID; profile name edits cannot change the protected slug. Registration retains the existing name-plus-ULID-suffix strategy and bounds new slugs to the existing 160-character column, including duplicate and long names. No migration, old-slug rewrite, or data deletion. Evidence: combined gallery/slug/type/registration Pest passed 49 tests / 340 assertions, focused PHPStan passed with no errors at 512MB, Pint and `git diff --check` passed. Playwright passed actual legacy-link redirects, generated-link clicks, name-edit continuity, and EN desktop/AR mobile flows (3 tests including setup and type regression).

No migration, old-slug rewrite, or data deletion. Playwright passed actual legacy-link redirects, generated-link clicks, name-edit continuity, and EN desktop/AR mobile flows (3 tests including setup and type regression).

## VEND-02 — P2 — Show a vendor-only public slider.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:64](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L64); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The public Blade page did not render the profile's existing `portfolio` media. It now renders only the active vendor's media, uses available large/thumb conversions with original-file fallback, and reuses the existing gallery interaction for thumbnails and wrapping Previous/Next buttons. Empty galleries are hidden; a single image has no unnecessary controls. Narrowed the existing vendor-hero CSS selector so it cannot darken the new gallery heading. No new package, media collection, schema, or production upload. Evidence: the 49-test / 340-assertion combined Pest run includes ownership and zero/one/multiple-image coverage in both locales; Vite build passed with existing runtime-resolved asset notices; Playwright's final 3-test run passed actual image loading, keyboard and pointer navigation, ownership isolation, empty/single galleries, stable links, and viewport bounds. Screenshots were visually inspected at `tests/e2e/.results/vendor-gallery-en.png` and `vendor-gallery-ar.png`. Initial browser failure was isolated to local E2E media fixture placement because `public/storage` is a directory rather than a link; only test fixture placement was adjusted, not application storage settings or user files.

No new package, media collection, schema, or production upload. Evidence: the 49-test / 340-assertion combined Pest run includes ownership and zero/one/multiple-image coverage in both locales; Vite build passed with existing runtime-resolved asset notices; Playwright's final 3-test run passed actual image loading, keyboard and pointer navigation, ownership isolation, empty/single galleries, stable links, and viewport bounds. Screenshots were visually inspected at `tests/e2e/.results/vendor-gallery-en.png` and `vendor-gallery-ar.png`. Initial browser failure was isolated to local E2E media fixture placement because `public/storage` is a directory rather than a link; only test fixture placement was adjusted, not application storage settings or user files.

## VEND-03 — P1 — Count and show only service types with real services.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:66](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L66); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The page previously counted approved type grants even without published services. The existing browsing repository now groups that vendor's displayable services across all pages, and the Blade type badges, type count, service count/list, and vendor index search/count use the same published-service scope. Deleting the last service of a type removes its badge and decreases both counts; drafts and other vendors do not contribute. The scope supports `catalog.digital_services_enabled`; DIG-01 now defaults this switch to false for the current phase; tests explicitly enable digital when verifying all three types. No service records or subscriptions were changed. Evidence: combined Pest passed 49 tests / 340 assertions, including EN/AR and digital enabled/disabled, 13 rentals spanning pagination, sale deletion, and approved-only empty state. Focused PHPStan/Pint and `git diff --check` passed; final combined Playwright passed 3 tests, and a separate digital-disabled Playwright run passed 2 tests including setup and real deletion/reload/count checks.

## WIZ-01 — P2 — Make event end date and time optional.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:68](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L68); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The database and resources already supported null ends, but the wizard HTML/JavaScript, planner search, draft requests, and DTO required them; parsing a missing end would otherwise substitute the current time. The wizard and EN/AR copy now mark end optional, keep future start required, reject malformed or nonpositive supplied ranges, and pass null unchanged through web/API requests, DTO and persistence. Offset dates are stored in UTC. Rentals retain a concrete per-item inventory window using their existing stored positive default duration only when no explicit item/event end exists; the event end remains null. Missing rental defaults are rejected with localized guidance, not invented durations. The same fallback covers customer-accepted rental additions; vendor conflict warnings use the saved item end and include active reservations without an attached item. No schema/data migration or old-record rewrite. API request/resource examples, registry and Bruno draft request were updated. Evidence: final focused Pest passed 53 tests / 248 assertions across EN/AR, rental/sale/digital, omitted/null/empty end, explicit end, invalid ranges, UTC, overlapping inventory, modification additions and vendor warnings. Focused PHPStan passed with no errors at 512MB; Pint and `git diff --check` passed; Vite build passed with existing asset notices. Playwright passed 3 tests including setup, existing checkout and actual start-only search/checkout in EN desktop and AR mobile for all three types; stored null and rental windows were checked against the isolated database. Screenshots `tests/e2e/.results/planner-optional-end-en.png` and `planner-optional-end-ar.png` were inspected. Earlier E2E API-cookie authentication failure was corrected in the test without weakening auth; the new vendor warning regression exposed and fixed the existing SQL NULL exclusion.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P2 — Make event end date and time optional. (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: The database and resources already supported null ends, but the wizard HTML/JavaScript, planner search, draft requests, and DTO required them; parsing a missing end would otherwise substitute the current time. ## WIZ-02 — P2 — Replace the Who is calling field with Notes.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:70](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L70); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Replaced the actual storefront "Who is celebrating?" field with a localized multiline Notes textarea. The existing `booking_customer_notes` text table now receives notes through a customer-owned, transaction-protected append action reused by draft creation and cart additions; identical latest notes are deduplicated, changed notes append without overwriting history, and failed service additions roll back note writes. Legacy celebrant columns and records remain intact. Browsing-only wizard submission uses the new web `POST wizard/context` flow to retain notes in the session without exposing private or long text in search URLs; successful checkout clears that session context. Cart, customer booking detail, booking API resources, and the owning vendor's detail/decision pages show escaped multiline notes. Validation accepts up to 10,000 characters and rejects longer input without truncation or partial writes. No new package, schema migration, or old-record rewrite. Existing API request/resource documentation, registry, Bruno example, and Discovery code map were updated. Evidence: final focused Pest passed 73 tests / 395 assertions, including EN/AR, 10,000-character preservation, oversized rejection, exact API round-trip, append history, rollback, customer/vendor ownership, and escaped script text. Focused PHPStan passed on 14 files at 512MB; Pint and `git diff --check` passed; agent-doc validation passed (7 documents, 69 local links, 17 modules). Combined Playwright passed 4 tests including setup, actual rental/sale/digital wizard submissions, multiline notes surviving cart reload, and search-session persistence in both locales. The final vendor-specific Playwright rerun passed 2 tests including setup and EN desktop/AR mobile multiline rendering; screenshots `tests/e2e/.results/vendor-event-notes-en.png` and `vendor-event-notes-ar.png` were inspected. Browser verification exposed unavailable Filament whitespace utility styling; applying pre-wrap only to the escaped note content fixed both collapsed lines and template-indentation spacing. Planner textarea screenshots were also inspected before the vendor rerun reset test artifacts. Focused evidence is complemented by the final local regression matrix below; production acceptance is not claimed.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P2 — Replace the Who is calling field with Notes. (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: Replaced the actual storefront "Who is celebrating?" field with a localized multiline Notes textarea. The existing `booking_customer_notes` text table now receives notes through a customer-owned, transaction-protected append action reused by draft creation and cart additions; identical latest notes are deduplicated, changed notes append without overwriting history, and failed service additions roll back note writes. Legacy celebrant columns and records remain intact. Cart, customer booking detail, booking API resources, and the owning vendor's detail/decision pages show escaped multiline notes. Existing API request/resource documentation, registry, Bruno example, and Discovery code map were updated. ## PKG-03 — P1 — Add an Occasion-dependent Package filter.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:76](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L76); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Added Package immediately after Occasion in the search sidebar, backed by the existing `PackageRecommendationReader` and package ULIDs. The server exposes only published packages linked to the selected occasion, rejects stale cross-occasion selections, and narrows the rendered package cards when one package is selected. The browser refreshes the options immediately and clears the prior value whenever Occasion changes; no second taxonomy or API endpoint was introduced. Evidence: focused Pest passed (7 tests, 39 assertions), Pint passed, Vite production build passed (with the repository's existing runtime-resolved asset notices), and Playwright passed card selection plus the dependent filter behavior in English desktop and Arabic mobile (3 tests including setup).

## HOME-01 — P1 — Reorder the homepage party builder and remove Category there only.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:78](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L78); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The homepage quick builder now renders Occasion → Governorate → City/Area → Date and Time, removes Category only from this form, loads active governorates from the existing geography repository, and reuses the established city endpoint and dependency behavior so City stays disabled and is cleared until a Governorate is selected. The date control now captures `event_starts_at` with `datetime-local`, and all valid values arrive selected on the existing search page. Evidence: focused Pest passed (4 tests, 33 assertions), Pint passed, and Playwright passed actual dependent-city loading and form submission in English desktop plus Arabic mobile (2 tests including setup). The first E2E attempt was invalidated only by test-factory country duplication; after binding the fixture to the seeded country, the rerun passed.

## PKG-04 — P1 — Integrate packages into the search backend.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:80](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L80); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Package recommendations now derive their eligible category IDs from the exact canonical service-search database query, so they inherit published lifecycle, text, product type, category, occasion, vendor, governorate/city coverage, price, rating, and rental availability constraints instead of maintaining parallel filter SQL. Exact event start/end timestamps are used for overlap checks when supplied; date-only searches retain day-level behavior. Packages are then constrained to occasions linked to the matching service categories, and a selected package is shown only when still eligible. Evidence: focused Pest passed (11 tests, 69 assertions), Pint passed, Playwright passed package click/filter plus deterministic city and availability changes (4 tests including setup), `git diff --check` passed, and focused PHPStan passed with no errors at 512MB. The first PHPStan attempt was incomplete only because the configured 128MB worker limit was exhausted; the higher-memory rerun completed cleanly.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P1 — Integrate packages into the search backend. (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: Package recommendations now derive their eligible category IDs from the exact canonical service-search database query, so they inherit published lifecycle, text, product type, category, occasion, vendor, governorate/city coverage, price, rating, and rental availability constraints instead of maintaining parallel filter SQL. Packages are then constrained to occasions linked to the matching service categories, and a selected package is shown only when still eligible. The first PHPStan attempt was incomplete only because the configured 128MB worker limit was exhausted; the higher-memory rerun completed cleanly.
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: vendor; candidate modules: Catalog, Discovery. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.

## PKG-05 — P2 — Display a fixed package price as one EGP amount.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:82](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L82); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Package recommendation reads now expose a single `fixed_budget` only when the stored minimum and maximum minor-unit values are equal, using Brick Money for the one-time minor-unit conversion. Homepage, search, and package-detail cards render that amount once without From/To wording while preserving range and starting-price behavior for non-fixed packages; homepage cards also use the public package-details route. Evidence: focused Pest passed (6 tests, 41 assertions), the complete package Playwright spec passed in both locales and across filtering/availability scenarios (4 tests including setup), Pint passed, focused PHPStan passed with no errors at 512MB, and `git diff --check` passed.

## REG-01 — P2 — Add optional vendor social and website URLs.

- Reproduction conditions reported: Added nullable Facebook, website, and Instagram URL columns through a module migration, then wired them through registration rules/DTO/action, API profile reads/updates, and vendor/admin profile forms. Empty strings normalize to null, omitted update fields remain unchanged, explicit null clears a link, and invalid links are rejected before registration or profile mutation. Labels and URL feedback are localized in EN/AR; Scribe field/response examples, the API registry, and Bruno examples cover the changed contract. Evidence: focused registration/social/profile Pest passed (24 tests, 208 assertions), the final social regression rerun passed (10 tests, 106 assertions), Pint passed, focused PHPStan passed (including the final update-action check with `--debug` after a parallel-worker timeout), and Playwright passed real registration plus profile editing/reload in both locales (3 tests including setup). The migration was verified on isolated test databases; deployment still needs the normal migration step. Earlier browser attempts exposed test timeout/locale setup and verification-fixture errors, all corrected before the green rerun.
8. Assertions: pin the intended outcome above; add exact ownership/status/amount/content assertions relevant to that record. A 200, visible heading or table alone cannot establish correctness.
9. Persistence verification: compare the affected records against the snapshot; rejected mutations must change nothing, successful mutations must write the intended result exactly once. Read only the disposable database.

## REG-02 — P1 — Require a valid vendor phone number.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:88](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L88); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The request and registration action already required an E.164 phone, but the Filament regex incorrectly accepted a zero country-code prefix. All three entry points now share the same E.164 regex, with localized required/invalid/duplicate messages. Phone normalization removes separators only from string input, so malformed non-string requests return validation errors without a cast warning. Regression tests prove missing, empty, local-only, zero-prefix, short, long, and malformed inputs cannot create a user or profile; valid spaced numbers still register. Evidence: focused registration/social Pest passed (40 tests, 287 assertions), Pint passed, focused PHPStan passed with `--debug` and 512MB, PHP request syntax and `git diff --check` passed, and Playwright passed invalid-phone feedback followed by corrected registration and profile editing in EN/AR (3 tests including setup).
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P1 — Require a valid vendor phone number. (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: The request and registration action already required an E.164 phone, but the Filament regex incorrectly accepted a zero country-code prefix. Regression tests prove missing, empty, local-only, zero-prefix, short, long, and malformed inputs cannot create a user or profile; valid spaced numbers still register. Evidence: focused registration/social Pest passed (40 tests, 287 assertions), Pint passed, focused PHPStan passed with `--debug` and 512MB, PHP request syntax and `git diff --check` passed, and Playwright passed invalid-phone feedback followed by corrected registration and profile editing in EN/AR (3 tests including setup).
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: vendor; candidate modules: Identity. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.

## REG-03 — P1 — Enforce vendor approval before portal access.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:90](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L90); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The existing API `vendor.approved` middleware and operational-page guard were not consistently applied to the vendor service surface: the services list, category browser, and resource access checks could still be reached directly before approval, and the onboarding checklist linked pending vendors to an unavailable service operation. These entry points now require `VendorOperationalAccess` (approved profile plus verified email and phone); stale product-type grants cannot bypass the profile gate. Pending, rejected, and changes-requested vendors receive 403 for service operations and the API returns the structured `vendor_approval_required` error; approved but unverified vendors receive `phone_verification_required`. Profile, documents, business-hours, coverage and phone-verification pages remain available for completing onboarding. Evidence (2026-10-05): focused approval/portal Pest passed 17 tests / 188 assertions for pending, rejected, changes-requested, approved and approved-unverified states in EN/AR; the related category/sale/price regressions passed 106 tests / 1,382 assertions. Scoped PHPStan passed with no errors; Pint and `git diff --check` passed. Actual Playwright passed setup plus one EN desktop/AR mobile scenario covering direct URLs and onboarding preservation. No migration or stored-data rewrite; final local regression evidence is recorded below.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P1 — Enforce vendor approval before portal access. (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: The existing API `vendor.approved` middleware and operational-page guard were not consistently applied to the vendor service surface: the services list, category browser, and resource access checks could still be reached directly before approval, and the onboarding checklist linked pending vendors to an unavailable service operation. Profile, documents, business-hours, coverage and phone-verification pages remain available for completing onboarding. Scoped PHPStan passed with no errors; Pint and `git diff --check` passed. Actual Playwright passed setup plus one EN desktop/AR mobile scenario covering direct URLs and onboarding preservation. No migration or stored-data rewrite; final local regression evidence is recorded below.

## REG-06 — P1 — Complete profile and documents after approval.

- Reproduction conditions reported: The existing approval action still requires the full eligibility checklist, while approved vendors can edit profile, account, documents, hours and coverage from the same portal session. The gap was the dashboard checklist hiding itself immediately after approval, which removed the resumable path for post-approval document/profile work and first-service setup. `VendorOnboardingChecklistWidget::canView()` now reuses the existing checklist for approved vendors and remains visible while any actionable step is incomplete; service operations remain behind `VendorOperationalAccess` and the approval/eligibility rules are unchanged. ## VDASH-01 — P2 — Replace raw keys on vendor dashboard cards.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:100](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L100); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The rental/sale/digital cards generated `vendor-portal.dashboard.services_by_type.*` keys that had no translations; added nonempty EN/AR labels for all three without changing their underlying counts or financial calculations. Recent-booking status badges now use the existing `booking.vendor_status.*` translations instead of deriving English text from enum values; filled the missing English `timed_out` label. Regression tests reject raw keys/state names, check all vendor-status translations, and render actual cards and a timed-out booking in both locales. Evidence: combined focused Pest passed 19 tests / 161 assertions including the static Filament translation gate and document lifecycle regressions. Pint and PHP syntax checks passed; `git diff --check` passed. Filament paths remain excluded by the configured PHPStan, so no widget PHPStan coverage is claimed. Actual Playwright dashboard journey passed in EN desktop/AR mobile, verified one service of each type, the localized status, no exposed translation/state keys, and no page-wide horizontal overflow. `tests/e2e/.results/vendor-dashboard-labels-en.png` and `vendor-dashboard-labels-ar.png` were inspected. No route/schema/package change; Digital visibility is handled by DIG-01.

## VDOC-01 — P1 — Repair the vendor documents navigation route.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:102](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L102); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Reproduced an actual 404 on the correctly generated `/vendor-portal/vendor-documents-page` URL: each row eagerly called `GenerateDocumentSignedUrlAction` while rendering, so one missing private file aborted the entire list and generated unnecessary signed-link audit entries for ordinary page reads. The existing Filament View action now invokes that same ownership/path/expiry-checked action only on explicit activation, redirects to the signed URL for an existing file, and reports a localized unavailable-file notification for 404 without breaking the table. Other HTTP failures, including forbidden access, are not swallowed. The existing route, page slug, panel middleware, vendor-only query, private file storage and approval lifecycle remain unchanged; no replacement file/data or new route was invented. Evidence: final focused Pest passed 19 tests / 161 assertions across navigation, guest/customer denial, owned/foreign row isolation, missing-file handling, explicit owned-file open and single audit write, private-path safeguards, document lifecycle/upload, dashboard labels and translation coverage. Pint, PHP syntax checks and `git diff --check` passed; focused PHPStan passed for the existing signed-URL action at 512MB, not the excluded Filament page. Final combined Playwright passed 3 tests including setup and actual EN desktop/AR mobile dashboard/documents journeys. The documents test clicked the native language switch and navigation, verified RTL/LTR and own-only records, activated the unavailable-file action, then reloaded the direct route with 200. Screenshots `tests/e2e/.results/vendor-documents-en.png` and `vendor-documents-ar.png` were inspected. Earlier browser attempts exposed isolated-factory geography duplication and locale/sidebar setup races; the final run uses seeded geography, the real language switch, and waits for its existing sidebar initialization before a normal click. Cross-reference: `BUG-VDOC-001` in the earlier 73-record ledger; no unrelated old records or totals were closed. Final local suite evidence is recorded below; production acceptance is not claimed.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P1 — Repair the vendor documents navigation route. (reported change target; not independent business approval)

## PHONE-01 — P1 — Enforce phone verification after approval.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:104](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L104); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Approval requires a phone number and verified email; approved vendors must verify their phone before operations or receiving-booking APIs, while profile/documents/hours/coverage and verification remain reachable. Identity now binds the real SMS Misr adapter outside local/testing, with five-minute hashed codes, five-guess limit, atomic single use, random staging/production codes and localized provider failures. No schema, credentials or production settings changed. Evidence: approval lifecycle/portal Pest 17 tests / 188 assertions, Chromium approval journey 2 tests with approved-unverified access in EN/AR, and SMS gateway/phone page Pest 12 tests / 44 assertions covering success, replacement, expiry, replay, wrong phone, guess limits, provider rejection, missing credentials, password-reset separation and environment bindings. Real delivery remains a deployment check requiring SMS Misr credentials and approved sender, not an unimplemented binding.

## SERV-DETAIL-01 — P2 — Remove four nonrequired properties from customer service detail.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:108](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L108); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Removed Location from the customer detail's booking facts, vendor preview and attribute component, and removed Made to order, Perishable and Stock from the sale attributes. Empty attribute sections and empty booking-fact groups are no longer rendered. Stored detail values and the stock-based disabled booking button are preserved. Evidence: final focused purchase/account/loyalty/search Pest passed (25 tests, 149 assertions), Pint and `git diff --check` passed, and Playwright passed rental/sale/digital detail pages plus sold-out empty-state rendering in English desktop and Arabic mobile (2 tests including setup). No route, schema or stored-data change. An initial render failure exposed the remaining vendor-preview reference, which was removed before the green rerun; the persistence test was corrected to compare database-hydrated values on both sides.

## SERV-FORM-01 — P1 — Enforce Main Category then Subcategory.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:112](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L112); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The existing shared category/occasion concern now supplies dependent main/subcategory fields to all three vendor resources. Main choices are active, type-compatible roots; the disabled subcategory field becomes available only after selecting a main category, shows its active/type-compatible direct children, and clears on parent changes. Server-side form rules reject missing, mismatched, nested-main, inactive, soft-deleted and wrong-type categories during both create and edit. Main category is form-only; the existing persisted category ID stores the selected child, with no new schema/API/DTO contract. Edit forms hydrate the pair; historical root-category services are not reclassified when opened, show EN/AR guidance, and require a valid child before saving. Actual edit validation revealed a separate 403: the vendor session guard selected a different permission namespace from the seeded web permissions. Service policy helpers now explicitly use the existing web namespace, and own-service checks also require the actor's vendor profile to own the record. Foreign edit URLs remain non-enumerating 404s and users without update permission remain forbidden. Evidence (2026-10-05): combined focused Pest passed 64 tests / 717 assertions, including 51 category/authorization cases, sale access, translation coverage, catalog security and Excel regressions. Pint and PHP syntax checks passed; focused PHPStan passed for the changed ServicePolicy (Filament paths remain excluded), and git diff --check passed. Combined actual Playwright passed 3 tests including setup and the existing sale access journey; the category journey created, edited and reloaded six services across rental/sale/digital, EN desktop and AR mobile using native form controls, and verified cleared stale children and refreshed occasion guidance. All six tests/e2e/.results/vendor-category-pair-{rental,sale,digital}-{en,ar}.png screenshots were inspected. The repaired full-marketplace journey includes a real subcategory linked to Birthday and passed through service publication and payment creation. Final regression evidence is recorded below. Category selection does not rewrite historical application data.

## SERV-FORM-02 — P1 — Accept and display service prices in EGP.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:114](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L114); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The existing native Filament price boundary remains the source for rental/sale/digital prices and now the booking invoice path renders the stored minor-unit values without re-conversion. Added a printable admin invoice route with service lines, vendor delivery, governorate transport base, each auditable transportation adjustment, and the final total; the admin booking page links to it and exposes the same breakdown. Evidence: prior focused price suite passed 108 tests / 1,310 assertions with EN/AR browser coverage; new transport/invoice Pest passed 2 tests / 13 assertions, including Brick Money totals, printed transport lines and source audit records. Pint and `git diff --check` passed. No PDF or tax-authority integration is claimed; the invoice output is the verified HTML print surface. The repaired full-marketplace browser journey passed; final local matrix evidence is recorded below.

## SERV-FORM-03 — P1 — Replace obsolete rental fields with dimensions.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:116](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L116); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Added nullable length_m, width_m and height_m to the existing rental-detail table through an additive migration; the explicit unit is metres and values roundtrip as two-decimal strings. Unknown historical dimensions stay null, never inferred from minimum floor space. Rental vendor create/edit forms now expose EN/AR Length, Width and Height instead of teardown, deposit and minimum-space inputs. Removed legacy fields are also excluded from vendor-page payloads, so normal saves and injected stale form state cannot erase historical values. Existing columns, admin forms, API fields, deposit currency and data remain intact. Reused the existing transactional create/update actions and extended rental cloning; no replacement model, dependency or financial representation was introduced. Native Laravel/Filament validation rejects zero, negative, exponent, malformed, over-precision and out-of-range dimensions; clearing a value stores null. Existing rental API create/partial update and vendor/customer resources expose the optional dimensions; omission preserves values, null clears a dimension, and older clients remain compatible. API registry, field PHPDocs, response fragment and two Bruno requests were updated; no routes were added. Customer service attributes show only known dimensions with localized units; sale/digital receive no rental fields. Evidence (2026-10-05): final combined focused Pest passed 158 tests / 1771 assertions across dimensions, price, categories/authorization, translations, Excel import and catalog security. The 50-test dimension suite includes isolated schema rollback/re-upgrade preservation, stale-form injection, clone, API compatibility and EN/AR rental/sale/digital presentation. Pint, syntax checks, scoped PHPStan for nine changed non-Filament files and git diff --check passed; Filament remains outside PHPStan coverage. Actual Chromium passed 3 tests including setup and sale access, with native rental creation/edit/clear/reload plus customer details in EN desktop/AR mobile. Four tests/e2e/.results/{vendor,customer}-rental-dimensions-{en,ar}.png screenshots were inspected. Migration ran only on disposable SQLite test databases, not the application's database or production; it must be applied during the authorized rollout, and MySQL migration acceptance remains unverified. The repaired full-marketplace browser journey passed; final local matrix evidence is recorded below.

## SERV-FORM-04 — P1 — Drive service forms and cards from category field schemas.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:118](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L118); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Shared vendor forms now render category-owned fields from `CategoryFieldSchema` through `CategoryFieldValidationService`, including inherited parent schemas and nearest-child overrides, with localized labels/options and all supported field types. Create/update actions, API requests, and Excel imports validate the saved values server-side (including required fields even when no values are submitted); customer presenters/cards/details expose only applicable localized attributes. Evidence (2026-10-05): focused Catalog tests passed 200 tests / 1,945 assertions, including schema inheritance, localized validation, API/action enforcement, Excel mapping, category/authorization, prices, dimensions, and all three service types. Actual Chromium passed 2 tests (EN desktop and AR mobile) covering inflatable-style rental fields, performer name, sale select field, digital boolean field, create/edit/reload persistence, and customer detail presentation. Pint, scoped PHPStan and `git diff --check` passed. No scattered page-level conditional or data rewrite was introduced; the focused full-marketplace browser journey passed; final matrix evidence is recorded below.

## SALE-01 — P1 — Verify and complete direct sale-service creation.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:120](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L120); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Reproduced the native `VendorSaleServiceResource` flow and confirmed `CreateVendorSaleService` creates and edits a sale service directly without Excel. The sale form now applies the selected category's dynamic schema (including required select values), while Excel remains optional and uses the same validator/mapping contract. ## SALE-02 — P0 — Repair the authorized vendor sale-service 403.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:122](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L122); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The reported 403 is not reproducible when the vendor satisfies the existing authorization contract: verified account, approved vendor profile, and active sale-type approval. `VendorSaleServiceResource::canCreate()` allows that vendor and continues to deny a vendor without sale approval. Added focused PHP authorization coverage plus a real Chromium login/direct-navigation regression for `/vendor-portal/vendor-sale-services/create`; the page returns 200 and renders the bilingual form. No application authorization was weakened or database change required. Evidence: Pest 2 tests / 4 assertions and Playwright 2 tests passed (setup plus focused sale access). The repaired full marketplace browser journey passed 2 tests including setup, reaching sale publication, booking submission/acceptance/confirmation and payment creation.

## DIG-01 — P2 — Hide digital services for the current phase.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:124](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L124); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Added `catalog.digital_services_enabled` with a false default for the current phase and documented `CATALOG_DIGITAL_SERVICES_ENABLED=false` in the example environment; all-type automated journeys explicitly opt in. Applied the switch consistently to customer search, categories, suggestions, similar services, vendor browsing, public detail/availability entry points, vendor dashboard type cards, service lists/resources, category browsers, digital creation/update/clone/import actions, import templates/history, and direct vendor API access. Digital records and import history remain stored; only operational/public visibility is filtered or denied while disabled, and re-enabling restores access. Evidence (2026-10-05): focused Pest passed 2 tests / 17 assertions for customer and vendor off/on behavior; related search, vendor-type, approval-gate and sale-authorization regressions passed 20 tests / 242 assertions. PHP syntax checks and Pint passed; `git diff --check` passed. Dashboard EN/AR off/on coverage also passed 2 tests / 81 assertions. The final digital-disabled browser run passed 3 tests including setup (1.1m), verifying public type counts, deletion/empty state and hidden digital dashboard/navigation in both locales. No migration, deletion, package change, or production configuration change.

## BOOK-01 — P0 — Repair vendor booking-detail 404.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:130](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L130); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Fixed the detail render crash by importing the existing `DeadlineColumnFormatter` helper in `VendorBookingDetailPage`. Also replaced MySQL-only `ISNULL(response_deadline)` ordering in the unified and incoming vendor booking lists with a portable `CASE WHEN ... IS NULL` expression, preserving nulls-last/deadline-ascending behavior and allowing the isolated SQLite browser flow to render the row action. The generated URL uses the `BookingVendor` public ULID; an owned record returns 200 while another vendor's record returns 403. Evidence: focused Pest 2 tests / 4 assertions and Playwright 2 tests passed (setup plus real vendor login, booking-list row link, and detail-page navigation).

## CHECKOUT-01 — P0 — Repair Save and Continue in booking checkout.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:132](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L132); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: The authenticated customer flow already reached the existing `CartController::setup` and `AddServiceToEventAction`, but a downstream item-validation failure could leave a newly created draft booking and address behind because draft creation and item addition were not atomic. Wrapped the complete draft lookup/create plus item addition in one transaction and locked an existing draft before reuse, while preserving the current user-facing validation response. Added focused success and out-of-stock rollback coverage: success creates one booking/address/vendor/item and redirects to the cart; failure creates none. Guest checkout is not currently supported because this route requires an authenticated customer, so no guest behavior was invented. Evidence: focused Pest 2 tests / 14 assertions and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus a real customer flow through Save and Continue on English desktop and Arabic mobile). No migration or stored-data rewrite was required.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: P0 — Repair Save and Continue in booking checkout. (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: The authenticated customer flow already reached the existing `CartController::setup` and `AddServiceToEventAction`, but a downstream item-validation failure could leave a newly created draft booking and address behind because draft creation and item addition were not atomic. Evidence: focused Pest 2 tests / 14 assertions and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus a real customer flow through Save and Continue on English desktop and Arabic mobile). Evidence: focused Pest 2 tests / 14 assertions and Playwright Chromium 2 tests passed (setup plus a real customer flow through Save and Continue on English desktop and Arabic mobile). No migration or stored-data rewrite was required.

## SHIP-01 — P1 — Add governorate transport pricing and an invoice adjustment.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:140](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L140); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Added integer minor-unit EGP transport fees to governorates, snapshots the selected address governorate fee on the booking, and includes that snapshot plus existing vendor delivery in booking totals. Admin booking users can add an explicit transportation line with a required source/reference; each line is append-only, linked to the booking, included in totals, shown separately in the printable invoice and resource breakdown, and recorded in `audit_logs`. Governorate editing now converts displayed EGP to stored minor units with Brick Money, while both transport inputs reject more than two decimal places with EN/AR feedback. Existing coverage-area delivery fees and saved booking fee snapshots remain intact. Evidence: `GovernorateTransportPriceFormTest`, `BookingTransportPricingTest` and bilingual `BookingInvoiceTest` verify persistence, precision, totals, transport lines, authorization and audit references. The actual finance browser journey passed 2 tests including setup (1.1m): admin price 25.00 → 37.50 persisted, the existing invoice retained its original 25.00 base plus 10.00 adjustment, and its total remained 150.00. Invoice locale middleware supports both languages; both A4 invoice outputs were rendered and visually inspected. Screenshots are under `docs/qa/screenshots/handoff-2026-10-05/finance/`. New migrations are additive; no application or production migration was run, and production database acceptance remains a deployment gate.

- Reproduction conditions reported: The vendor wallet table now starts with Date, Party Number, Party Total, Commission Percentage, and Party Net, then retains direction/amount/running balance/description. Party values come from the linked Commission → BookingItem → BookingVendor → Booking chain, use Brick Money (including subtotal plus delivery), and show an em dash for non-party ledger rows. ## WALLET-02 — P1 — Add a printable vendor statement below Withdrawals.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:146](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L146); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Added `VendorStatementPage` at finance sort 3, backed only by the authenticated vendor's append-only wallet ledger. It shows date, operation reference, credit/debit, amount and a declared oldest-to-newest running balance; missing projections are reconciled from ledger rows. The statement disables pagination so printing includes every row. Print CSS hides the sidebar and its overlay, which otherwise dimmed the A4 page when responsive breakpoints changed. Evidence: focused `VendorWalletStatementTest` passed 2 tests / 6 assertions for owned rows, running-balance reconciliation and foreign isolation. The finance browser journey verifies menu order, all 13 owned rows, oldest-first order, final 81.00 balance, foreign isolation, the Print button and hidden sidebar/overlay in both locales. The statement disables pagination so printing includes every row. Print CSS hides the sidebar and its overlay, which otherwise dimmed the A4 page when responsive breakpoints changed. Evidence: focused `VendorWalletStatementTest` passed 2 tests / 6 assertions for owned rows, running-balance reconciliation and foreign isolation. The finance browser journey verifies menu order, all 13 owned rows, oldest-first order, final 81.00 balance, foreign isolation, the Print button and hidden sidebar/overlay in both locales. EN/AR A4 outputs were rendered and visually inspected with all five columns visible. Screenshots are under `docs/qa/screenshots/handoff-2026-10-05/finance/`. No new schema or ledger mutation.

## LOYAL-01 — P2 — Hide Loyalty Points from the vendor portal.

- Original report: [docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md:150](../../docs/qa/instaparty-developer-handoff-backlog-2026-10-04.md#L150); type: handoff change / reported defect / decision.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Disabled the vendor loyalty page through Filament's existing `canAccess()` permission hook, which also removes its navigation, and guarded `save()` against a stale component invocation. Customer/platform pages, APIs, program records and points history are untouched; no migration or configuration change was made. Evidence: focused Pest passed (2 tests, 8 assertions), proving an approved vendor receives 403 and an existing program survives; Pint and `git diff --check` passed. Playwright verified missing navigation and direct-route 403 in English desktop and Arabic mobile, then confirmed authorized sale creation still opens (2 tests including setup). The first expanded browser case exceeded its original 30-second budget; a complete 120-second-budget rerun passed.

## TA-SYS-001 — TA-SYS-001 — Published service returned as unavailable in wishlist

- Original report: [docs/qa/tester-army-system/confirmed-findings.md:5](../../docs/qa/tester-army-system/confirmed-findings.md#L5); type: confirmed in prior campaign.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Severity: medium. Save the published sale fixture to `POST /api/v1/customer/wishlist/items`. Creation returns 201 and the correct public service ID, but `data.is_available` is false. The same service is published, stocked and displayable in discovery. Root cause: `app/Modules/Discovery/Http/Resources/WishlistItemResource.php:24` compares `Service.status` directly to the `ServiceStatus::Published` enum. `Service.php:106` casts the status to `ServiceState`, so the strict comparison fails. Repro: `tests/tester-army/bugs/wishlist-availability.e2e.ts`. Evidence: `.e2e/system-audit/repros/results/wishlist-availability-published-displayable-service-10555c5fea52f710/trace.md`.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: Published displayable service: wishlist is_available=true; correct service ID and persisted saved item.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: Severity: medium. Save the published sale fixture to `POST /api/v1/customer/wishlist/items`. Creation returns 201 and the correct public service ID, but `data.is_available` is false. The same service is published, stocked and displayable in discovery. Root cause: `app/Modules/Discovery/Http/Resources/WishlistItemResource.php:24` compares `Service.status` directly to the `ServiceStatus::Published` enum. `Service.php:106` casts the status to `ServiceState`, so the strict comparison fails. Repro: `tests/tester-army/bugs/wishlist-availability.e2e.ts`. Evidence: `.e2e/system-audit/repros/results/wishlist-availability-published-displayable-service-10555c5fea52f710/trace.md`.
- Impacted roles: unresolved until entry-route verification; wording-derived candidates: customer; candidate modules: Catalog, Discovery. Mentioning a vendor/customer does not establish that they are the executing actor.
- Required fixture data: disposable records matching the reported trigger above, including its lifecycle, age, ownership, localization, and monetary values; fields omitted by the report remain unresolved. Do not substitute empty fixtures for populated workflow records.
- Related scenario IDs: No exact source-file link established; unresolved, not invented. Matching source references are candidate traceability only.
- Current persistence: See pilot B01 in KNOWN_DEFECT_BENCHMARK.md; limited to tested branch.
- Related later report references: [docs/qa/tester-army-system-execution-2026-10-12.md](../../docs/qa/tester-army-system-execution-2026-10-12.md)

## TA-SYS-002 — TA-SYS-002 — Accepted chat report type reaches an unhandled server exception

- Original report: [docs/qa/tester-army-system/confirmed-findings.md:13](../../docs/qa/tester-army-system/confirmed-findings.md#L13); type: confirmed in prior campaign.
- Reproduction conditions reported: Severity: medium. An authenticated customer submits `reportable_type=chat_thread`, a valid-format public ULID that does not exist, `reason=other` and local test details. The expected outcome is a client error for the unsupported/missing target. Actual status is **500**, captured by the repeat assertion `expected 500 to be less than 500`. Root cause: `app/Modules/TrustSafety/Http/Requests/SubmitReportRequest.php:24` accepts `chat_thread`, while `SubmitReportAction.php:17` maps only `vendor_profile` and line 28 throws `InvalidArgumentException` for the accepted chat type. This confirms an input-handling failure, not a successful test of reports against real chat participants. Repro: `tests/tester-army/bugs/report-type.e2e.ts`. Evidence: `.e2e/system-audit/repros/results/report-type-unknown-chat-report-target-91b3566f01d265d5/trace.md`.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: Unsupported/missing report target: client error, never an unhandled 500; no report persisted.
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: Severity: medium. An authenticated customer submits `reportable_type=chat_thread`, a valid-format public ULID that does not exist, `reason=other` and local test details. The expected outcome is a client error for the unsupported/missing target. Actual status is **500**, captured by the repeat assertion `expected 500 to be less than 500`. Root cause: `app/Modules/TrustSafety/Http/Requests/SubmitReportRequest.php:24` accepts `chat_thread`, while `SubmitReportAction.php:17` maps only `vendor_profile` and line 28 throws `InvalidArgumentException` for the accepted chat type. This confirms an input-handling failure, not a successful test of reports against real chat participants. Repro: `tests/tester-army/bugs/report-type.e2e.ts`. Evidence: `.e2e/system-audit/repros/results/report-type-unknown-chat-report-target-91b3566f01d265d5/trace.md`.

## LOCAL-CLIENT-01 — 01 — One gallery source and actual storage checks

- Original report: [docs/qa/client-seven-requirements-2026-10-12.md:41](../../docs/qa/client-seven-requirements-2026-10-12.md#L41); type: client requirement / presentation change.
- Reproduction conditions reported: `VendorBrowsingRepository::portfolioFor()` previously aggregated only service images while the Blade controller read vendor-owned portfolio media. They now share one ordered source: vendor portfolio first, then galleries of displayable vendor-owned services. URLs are deduplicated. The existing API limit remains 24 while the web gallery can reach all valid images; `total_count` measures all valid unique images. `PublicMediaUrl::for()` checks the configured conversion disk for a generated conversion, then the original disk. Missing conversions fall back to existing originals; missing media is excluded without fabricating images or deleting data. The generic service card uses the same safe selection for its medium image. Runtime failures in the vendor gallery skip the failed image and reveal an empty state when none remain. Broken thumbnails are hidden. Files: `app/Modules/Shared/Application/Services/PublicMediaUrl.php`, `app/Modules/Discovery/Infrastructure/Repositories/VendorBrowsingRepository.php`, `app/Modules/Discovery/Http/Controllers/Web/VendorController.php`, `resources/views/storefront/vendors/show.blade.php`, `resources/js/storefront.js`, `resources/views/components/storefront/service-card.blade.php`, `lang/{en,ar}/storefront.php`. Storage limitation: this checkout has a copied `public/storage` directory rather than a link. Browser setup copies only its own synthetic QA SVG assets to the corresponding public paths, preserving the directory. Production must serve originals and conversions from the configured public disk/storage URL. PHP disk existence does not prove CDN access; post-deployment HTTP verification remains necessary. Unrecoverable missing originals require a backup/re-upload on the real server. Runtime recovery prevents broken icons but cannot recover lost bytes.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: 01 — One gallery source and actual storage checks (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: `VendorBrowsingRepository::portfolioFor()` previously aggregated only service images while the Blade controller read vendor-owned portfolio media. They now share one ordered source: vendor portfolio first, then galleries of displayable vendor-owned services. URLs are deduplicated. The existing API limit remains 24 while the web gallery can reach all valid images; `total_count` measures all valid unique images. `PublicMediaUrl::for()` checks the configured conversion disk for a generated conversion, then the original disk. Missing conversions fall back to existing originals; missing media is excluded without fabricating images or deleting data. The generic service card uses the same safe selection for its medium image. Runtime failures in the vendor gallery skip the failed image and reveal an empty state when none remain. Broken thumbnails are hidden. ## LOCAL-CLIENT-07 — 07 — Dependent public categories

- Original report: [docs/qa/client-seven-requirements-2026-10-12.md:83](../../docs/qa/client-seven-requirements-2026-10-12.md#L83); type: client requirement / presentation change.
- Reproduction conditions reported: SearchController validates `subcategory` against the active category list and selected parent. Its effective category code feeds the existing search DTO/query, vendor options and combined filters; no new search engine or schema is introduced. Old direct-child `category_slug` links normalize to parent plus child. Invalid, inactive or mismatched child selections fail validation. The category selector lists roots. Child options are localized server-side, then updated from safely encoded JSON by the existing storefront script. Changing a parent clears the child and disables the selector if it has no children. Vendor options refresh using the selected child. Reload, query-state pagination and active-filter removal preserve expected state. Files: `SearchController.php`, `resources/views/storefront/search.blade.php`, `resources/js/storefront.js`, `lang/{en,ar}/storefront.php`. Existing public APIs are documented in `docs/api/vendor-public-presentation.md`, `.specify/memory/api-registry.md` and three Bruno requests. Vendor-owned portfolio items have nullable `service_public_id`; consumers must handle this association. No new API route was introduced.
- Intended correct behavior / source oracle: 07 — Dependent public categories (reported change target; not independent business approval)
- Previously observed incorrect behavior / closure text: SearchController validates `subcategory` against the active category list and selected parent. Its effective category code feeds the existing search DTO/query, vendor options and combined filters; no new search engine or schema is introduced. Old direct-child `category_slug` links normalize to parent plus child. Invalid, inactive or mismatched child selections fail validation. The category selector lists roots. Child options are localized server-side, then updated from safely encoded JSON by the existing storefront script. Changing a parent clears the child and disables the selector if it has no children. Vendor options refresh using the selected child. Reload, query-state pagination and active-filter removal preserve expected state. Files: `SearchController.php`, `resources/views/storefront/search.blade.php`, `resources/js/storefront.js`, `lang/{en,ar}/storefront.php`. Existing public APIs are documented in `docs/api/vendor-public-presentation.md`, `.specify/memory/api-registry.md` and three Bruno requests. Independent reproduction design (source-bound, unexecuted unless selected in the pilot):

1. Preconditions: reproduce the reported trigger: SearchController validates `subcategory` against the active category list and selected parent. Its effective category code feeds the existing search DTO/query, vendor options and combined filters; no new search engine or schema is introduced. Old direct-child `category_slug` links normalize to parent plus child. Invalid, inactive or mismatched child selections fail validation. The category selector lists roots. Child options are localized server-side, then updated from safely encoded JSON by the existing storefront script. Changing a parent clears the child and disables the selector if it has no children. Vendor options refresh using the selected child. Reload, query-state pagination and active-filter removal preserve expected state.
